- **AC-HE-127 (auto, BLOCKING) No REACH through a wall, and the real passage read.** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* GIVEN the real `door_passage` read (Layout LY-28, D.5, AC-180) driven through Layout's `StubOpeningRead` at `area_max` 64, over fixtures for an E-W wall and a N-S wall, each with the leaf hinged left and hinged right, WHEN each is evaluated, THEN `gap_rect` lies on the side away from the hinge for a partial leaf, the four wall/hinge fixtures equal Layout AC-180's table, and the passable test requires `open_extent_px >= 16` across the E-W run and `open_extent_px >= 8` across the N-S run (the 12 px, 16 px and 6 px openings are area 8, 11 and 4, D.5: 12, 16, 6; a 12 px opening is passable across a N-S wall and not across an E-W wall; a 6 px opening is passable across neither; a 16 px opening across both); GIVEN a lit seat in room X, the horror in the adjacent room Y with the two foot points 40 px apart (inside `REACH_RANGE_PX` 96) across a one-tile wall, the phase BREACH and conditions (a) to (e) true, WHEN 600 ticks run, THEN no REACH begins and no limb pose or touch-down SMEAR exists; GIVEN the same pair with an open, passable leaf whose `gap_rect` the foot-to-P segment crosses, THEN a REACH begins on the first run (in each of the four wall and hinge fixtures); GIVEN the leaf open but the segment passing through the wall beside the gap or through the hinge-side part of a partial leaf, or the leaf closed, or its passage below the foot extent, THEN no REACH begins; the same fixtures hold for the anchor REACH. The standoff, the step-away and the HE-11 waypoint filter use `linked`, not `connected` (AC-HE-14 and AC-HE-16 run once with the wall and once with the open sill). *Covers HE-14 (f), HE-19, HE-11, E-HE-20, E-HE-35.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-HE-24 (auto) The draw.** GIVEN a REACH begins with `"horror.tell"` counter 7 and a stubbed `rng_int(19, 36)` of 22, WHEN the block is written, THEN `UNFOLD_TICKS` is 110, a multiple of 5 within [`UNFOLD_LO` 95, `UNFOLD_HI` 180]; `reach_end_T = reach_start_T + 110` is a multiple of 5; the counter reads 8 in the same block write; and the only random source is `rng_derive_epoch(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)` (the fixture pins `restore_epoch`). *Covers HE-14, HE-F5, HE-42.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: kernel name `rng_derive_epoch`)*
- **AC-HE-25 (auto) The strike publishes its facts on the run of the state change.** GIVEN a REACH that resolves, WHEN `horror.view` is read at the end of that run, THEN `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T` were published from the run that began the REACH (no one-run lag), `strike_T` and the seats hit are published on the STRIKE entry run, and a touch-down SMEAR exists at P from the run at `hold_start_T` and not before, while `reach_start_T` is published from the REACH-begin run *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *Covers HE-14, HE-15, HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-26 (auto + lint) No marker.** GIVEN a REACH in the hold stage, WHEN the scene tree under the world layer is enumerated for every node whose position lies within `R_SNAP_PX` 72 of P, THEN the set is exactly {the rig's reach-limb segment sprites, the touch-down SMEAR mark sprite} and any other node, or any node whose class or group name matches `*ring*`, `*arc*`, `*disc*`, `*decal*`, `*marker*`, `*reticle*` or `*lock*`, fails the test; the mark ring holds exactly one entry at P (kind SMEAR) from the run at `hold_start_T` (none before it) and none of any other kind; and a headless frame diff of the hold stage with the rig hidden against the same frame with the rig shown differs only inside the union of the limb's and the smear's bounding boxes (0 differing pixels outside them). *Covers HE-14, CP-1.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was "inspected", with no pass threshold)*
- **AC-HE-143 (auto) The hold-stage smear and the pre-hold fizzle.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN a REACH with `UNFOLD_TICKS` 110 (`hold_start_T = reach_end_T − 5 × floor(3 × 22 / 10)` = `reach_end_T − 30`), WHEN it runs to resolution, THEN no SMEAR exists at P before `hold_start_T`, exactly one exists from the run at `hold_start_T` (`reach_smear_laid` true), and `horror.view` has published `reach_start_T`, `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T` since the REACH-begin run; the gap `reach_end_T − hold_start_T` is 25 ticks at the draw 95 and 50 at the draw 180; GIVEN a fizzle 40 ticks into the REACH (phase to CALM) before the hold stage, THEN no smear is laid; GIVEN a fizzle after `hold_start_T`, THEN the smear stays; a restore one run before `hold_start_T` lays the smear once, and a restore after it does not lay a second (the flag persists). *Covers HE-14, HE-17, V3, V4.*
- **AC-HE-27 (auto) Strike geometry boundary.** GIVEN `R_SNAP_PX` 72, P = (400, 300) and seats at dx = 71, 72 and 73 px (axis-aligned) in the same room, one seat in the next room at 60 px across a wall, one seat in the adjacent room at 60 px across an open, passable sill (spared: the strike is strictly same-room, HE-15), and one DOWNED seat at 10 px, WHEN the strike resolves, THEN only the 71 px seat is hit (`71² = 5041 < 5184`; 72 gives `5184 < 5184` false); exactly one `apply_suit_damage(player_id, STRIKE_BP, source_ref)` call is made per hit seat in ascending player id; `source_ref` carries the EXOGENOUS class; the comparison uses integers only. *Covers HE-15, HE-F2, E-HE-1, E-HE-35.*
- **AC-HE-28 (auto, integration with Suit) The three-strike ladder.** GIVEN integrity 10000 and `STRIKE_BP` 3200, WHEN three strikes land, THEN integrity reads 6800 (STRAINED), 3600 (CRITICAL: stagger, walk-only) and 400 (FAILED, then DOWNED); a fourth strike is never issued on the DOWNED seat; a seat struck at 3600 ends at 400 and FAILED; and with no living non-DOWNED seat remaining, the target becomes ANCHOR: after the cooldown the record STALKs to the anchor and its REACHes call no `apply_suit_damage` (HE-13, HE-16 (3)); it is never in LURK while the phase is BREACH. GIVEN `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1, THEN the rung is set at the next `suit.vitals` run (at most 6 ticks after the hit) with no hold (SE-23). *Covers HE-15, HE-18, HE-F3, E-HE-2.*
- **AC-HE-29 (auto) The strike hold.** GIVEN a STRIKE, WHEN it resolves, THEN the state stays STRIKE for `STRIKE_HOLD_RUNS` 2 runs (10 ticks), begins on the first run with `T >= reach_end_T`, and a seat on a door sill belongs to the sill's room. *Covers HE-15.*
- **AC-HE-30 (auto) GOUGE subject and contact.** GIVEN a strike with P within `HULL_CONTACT_PX` 24 of a hull-segment face, WHEN it resolves, THEN a GOUGE is deposited on that segment and radial hairlines are laid at the contact point; a duct, cable, pipe or door is never a subject (lint `GOUGE_NON_HULL`). *Covers HE-16, FT-31.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: testable; Layout publishes `hull_segments_near`, C-HE-11 closed.)*
- **AC-HE-31 (auto) Epoch caps.** GIVEN four consecutive strikes at one hull face in one `tell_epoch`, WHEN each resolves, THEN at most `GOUGES_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 3 GOUGE and `RUPTURES_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 1 RUPTURE are deposited and all five epoch counters reset and `gouged_seg_ids` empties at the first run that sees the next `tell_epoch` (lazy reset, AC-HE-126). *Covers HE-16, HE-F17.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: testable; C-HE-11 closed.)*
- **AC-HE-32 (auto) The ladder gate.** GIVEN strike one on a fresh segment at T = 1075, strike two on the same segment at T = 1550 and strike three on it later, WHEN each resolves, THEN strike one deposits GOUGE, strike two deposits RUPTURE when the seat has walked out of the disc (the segment is in `gouged_seg_ids`, `ruptures_epoch 0 < 1`, empty hit list) and GOUGE when the seat stood and was struck, and strike three deposits GOUGE; one hull deposit kind per STRIKE; a RUPTURE never occurs on a segment not in `gouged_seg_ids`, in the same strike that gouged it, or on a strike that hit a seat *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *Covers HE-16, HE-F17, E-HE-12, E-HE-56.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review; the hull-segment lookup is now published, C-HE-11 closed.)*
- **AC-HE-133 (auto, integration with Suit and the Graph; BLOCKING) A CRITICAL seat and a hull breach.** GIVEN a seat at integrity 3600 (CRITICAL), staggered, standing in the disc of a REACH beside a hull face that carries this epoch's GOUGE from an earlier strike (the segment is in `gouged_seg_ids`), WHEN the REACH resolves, THEN `apply_suit_damage` is called once (to 400, FAILED), no RUPTURE is issued and `ruptures_epoch` stays 0; GIVEN the same REACH with the seat 5 ticks past the end of its stagger and outside the disc, THEN one RUPTURE is issued on the segment, the Graph raises its own breach clock, and a walk-only CRITICAL seat starting at the disc boundary reaches the nearest closable door (within the route budget `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` of DA-29) and closes it before the Graph's own decompression threshold (about 131 s = 7860 ticks at the initial hole, LSG:707, a figure to be re-cited on the confirming run); the assertion reads the Graph's published `failures()` and never a Horror-side estimate. *Covers HE-16, E-HE-56, HE-F16.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-33 (auto) Anchor REACH.** GIVEN no lit seat, WHEN the anchor REACH resolves, THEN the anchor is the lowest-id anchor of the locus section's outermost compartment within `HULL_CONTACT_PX` of a hull face; it lays hairlines and, with `ANCHOR_REACH_GOUGES` 1, one GOUGE; it never issues RUPTURE or `apply_suit_damage`; with no such anchor it lays hairlines only. *Covers HE-16 (3), E-HE-21.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: testable; C-HE-11 closed.)*
- **AC-HE-34 (auto) A dark seat beside the anchor is not harmed.** GIVEN a dark seat parked within `R_SNAP_PX` of the anchor at an anchor REACH's resolution, WHEN it resolves, THEN `apply_suit_damage` is not called and the hull takes the GOUGE. *Covers E-HE-22.*
- **AC-HE-35 (auto) Cooldown.** GIVEN a strike at T, WHEN the next REACH is attempted, THEN none begins before `T + STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS` 300; GIVEN a fizzle, THEN the next REACH may begin immediately; GIVEN `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS` 30 (at `STAGGER_TICKS`), THEN the data load fails (negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-17, HE-F15.*
- **AC-HE-36 (auto) Never what is already down.** GIVEN a seat DOWNED or DEAD at candidate selection, or DOWNED mid-REACH by another cause (a teammate's friendly harm), or IN_POD, WHEN the horror acts, THEN it is not a candidate and is not hit, and the REACH completes harmlessly to RETREAT; a STAGGERED seat remains a candidate. *Covers HE-13, HE-18, E-HE-18, E-HE-53.*
- **AC-HE-37 (auto + lint) Door leaf.** GIVEN a closed door leaf between the horror and P, WHEN the strike resolves, THEN no seat on the far side is hit, no `probe()` or physics query occurred in the run, and the segment test used the room-graph sill. *Covers HE-19, E-HE-19.*
- **AC-HE-38 (auto) Late lamp-off and layout change at resolution.** GIVEN the lamp is switched off after P is locked, THEN the strike still resolves at P and hits every living seat in the disc and only the next run's target changes; GIVEN a weld or cut on the resolving tick, THEN P is unchanged, the same-room and closed-leaf tests are re-evaluated against the current layout, and a layout change can only spare a seat. *Covers HE-19, E-HE-14, E-HE-23.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Modular Ship Assembly is `Status: Designed`; testable against MSA-14, MSA-20.)*
- **AC-HE-39 (auto) REACH from a cleared cooldown.** GIVEN a lit seat already within `REACH_RANGE_PX` at the run the cooldown clears, WHEN that run executes, THEN the REACH begins and its telegraph is at least `UNFOLD_LO`. *Covers E-HE-6.*
- **AC-HE-40 (auto) RETREAT.** GIVEN a STRIKE, WHEN RETREAT runs, THEN it moves at `V_CREEP_MPX` to the nearest waypoint at least `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` from every living seat; it goes to STALK when `T >= cooldown_until_T` in BREACH, to LURK in CALM or TELL when not returning; and a second TELL during RETREAT goes RETREAT, LURK, STALK, never skipping LURK, resetting `strikes_epoch`, `gouges_epoch`, `ruptures_epoch`, `chill_epoch_dj` and `chill_deposits_epoch` and emptying `gouged_seg_ids` lazily at the first run that sees the new `tell_epoch` (AC-HE-126). *Covers HE-17, E-HE-16.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

### Group P: presence, movement and counterplay (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-41 (auto) Ingress choice.** GIVEN a TELL whose `locus_body` is not the horror's body and seats at known `lux_pm` values, WHEN ingress completes, THEN the arrival hull segment lies in a room one hop from the room of the highest-`lux_pm` seat (none lit: the locus section's outermost compartment), in a room holding no living seat and at least `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` from every living seat; ties lowest hop, then lowest room id, then lowest hull-segment id. Door state comes from Layout's real `door_passage` (AC-HE-138 (f)); the row never reads NOT ASSESSED for want of the read *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *Covers HE-20, HE-F9.*
- **AC-HE-42 (auto) Ingress timing.** GIVEN TELL entry at T = 1200 with tell_len 900, WHEN ingress runs, THEN `transit_until_T` is 1500, the ingress mark (smear and hairlines) is laid at 1440 (`INGRESS_MARK_LEAD_TICKS` 60 before the body), the body registers at the run at 1500 and is in STALK, BREACH begins at 2100 and 600 ticks of stalk were published (at least `TELL_MIN_TICKS − INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` = 300); `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` 400 fails the relation (`400 + 240 + 60 = 700 > 600`, negative fixture, expected FAIL); `D_entry_max` is 492 px. *Covers HE-20, HE-F9.*
- **AC-HE-43 (auto) Registration order.** GIVEN an ingress, WHEN it runs, THEN E7 (egress mark at the old site) precedes E6 on arrival, E8 runs once per run, `S_h` stays 1, and a seat entering the chosen room during the mark-lead window forces a re-choice by the same rule while the first hairline stays. *Covers HE-20, HE-34, E-HE-34.*
- **AC-HE-44 (auto, negative fixture, expected FAIL) Cry-wolf and its fallback.** GIVEN no legal ingress entry at `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS`, a tell of `TELL_MIN_TICKS` (600) and a fallback entry available, (a) pinned fixture `tell_enter_T` = 1200 (T mod 30 = 0, so the BREACH entry is also a Horror run): `transit_until_T` = 1500, `fallback_T` = 1500 + 240 = 1740 (540 after TELL entry), the fallback entry (the nearest legal hull anchor, HE-F9) is chosen and its ingress mark is laid at the run at 1740, `arrive_T` = min{T >= 1740 + 60, T mod 5 = 0} = 1800 = the BREACH entry, the record registers (E6) on the run at 1800 and `horror_ingress_pending` is clear after that run; (b) unpinned fixture `tell_enter_T` = 1206 (a multiple of 6, T mod 5 = 1): `fallback_T` = 1746, the mark is laid at the first Horror run at or after it, 1750, BREACH entry is 1806, `arrive_T` = 1810 (the first multiple of 5 at or after 1746 + 60 = 1806; 1810 - 1750 = 60 ticks of mark lead kept), i.e. 4 ticks after BREACH entry, the maximum (E-HE-17, HE-4, AC-HE-125); in both fixtures the record is registered, in STALK, and `horror_ingress_pending` is clear after **the first Horror run at or after BREACH entry** (1800, 1810); GIVEN the fixture in which every hull room of the locus body holds a living seat, WHEN the record stays in TRANSIT, THEN `horror_ingress_pending` is raised, and a fixture whose record is still unregistered after the first Horror run at or after BREACH entry while a legal or fallback entry exists fails the build (negative fixture, expected FAIL). The relation `300 + 240 + 60 = 600 <= 600` holds with zero slack, so alignment can only delay registration by at most 4 ticks, never past the first Horror run that reads BREACH. *Covers HE-20, HE-F9, E-HE-33.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: run-grid alignment, E-HE-17)*
- **AC-HE-45 (auto) TRANSIT.** GIVEN a record in TRANSIT, WHEN runs execute, THEN it is unregistered, publishes no `pos_px`, lays no marks, harms nothing, and its arrival state follows the phase view (TELL and BREACH STALK, SUBSIDING RETREAT; a return home that completes in CALM arrives as LURK); **an abort at TRANSIT tick +100 (and an abort on the arrival run) cancels the arrival: no E6 call, no `pos_px`, no new mark, `transit_to_body_id` equals `from_body_id`, and the record is in the player's body at no tick** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. *Covers HE-20, E-HE-15.*
- **AC-HE-46 (auto) Return.** GIVEN `RETURN_HOME` 1, WHEN SUBSIDING ends or a TELL aborts, THEN after `RETURN_DELAY_TICKS` 600 the record returns to its home body by the HE-20 rule; GIVEN `RETURN_HOME` 0, THEN it remains and LURKs in the body of the last breach. *Covers HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-47 (auto) Dormancy.** GIVEN a record whose body unloads, WHEN 10000 ticks pass with no new `tell_epoch` whose locus body differs from the record's body and the body reloads, THEN the record is byte-identical to its state at unload, no mark aged and no disturbance ran; a TRANSIT record whose destination unloads waits. **"Byte-identical at reload" excludes the TELL departure of HE-20**: when a new `tell_epoch` arrives, the only permitted differences are `state` TRANSIT, the transit fields (`from_body_id`, `transit_to_body_id`, `transit_until_T`, `transit_leg`), `last_seen_tell_epoch`, `epoch_id`, the zeroed epoch counters, the cleared Emitter registration and one egress mark in the ring. *Covers HE-21, E-HE-43.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-125 (auto, BLOCKING) A told breach always brings the horror (cry-wolf fixture 1).** GIVEN derelict B (the home body) unloaded and the horror resident in it, a TELL on the home ship (`locus_body` is not B) pinned to a tick with `T mod 5 = 0`, a legal ingress entry and `RETURN_HOME` 1 in one run and 0 in another, WHEN the TELL runs, THEN on the first run after TELL entry the record is in TRANSIT with an egress mark in B's section, E7 is called only if the ref was still registered, and the record registers (E6) and is in STALK on the run at `tell_enter_T + INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` (300; at most 4 ticks later for an unpinned entry, E-HE-17) in a room one hop from the lit seat; the same fixture with the TELL on derelict A gives the same result; in both settings `horror_ingress_pending` is 0 and the BREACH that follows contains a registered record. *Covers HE-12, HE-20, HE-21, guard 2.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-126 (auto, BLOCKING) Lazy epoch reset (cry-wolf fixture 2).** GIVEN a record that holds `strikes_epoch` 2, `gouges_epoch` 3, `ruptures_epoch` 1, `chill_deposits_epoch` 24, `gouged_seg_ids` [S1, S2, S3] and `last_seen_tell_epoch` 4, with its body unloaded across two TELLs whose locus bodies are elsewhere and a test hook that suppresses the departure so the record stays resident, WHEN the body reloads and the record takes its first run at `tell_epoch` 6, THEN before any act in that run all five counters read 0, `gouged_seg_ids` is empty, `last_seen_tell_epoch` is 6, and a GOUGE and a RUPTURE are available again; GIVEN the same record restored from a save taken at `tell_epoch` 4 into a world at `tell_epoch` 6, THEN the same holds. *Covers the Epoch counters rule, HE-16, HE-41.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-48 (auto) Weld and cut.** GIVEN a weld mid-path and a cut removing the record's section, WHEN the next run executes, THEN the path cache is recomputed, `body_id` is re-derived from `body_of`, a record with no section goes TRANSIT with an egress mark, and `locus_body` is unchanged by the weld (only an AT-14 fallback, recorded as LOCUS_FALLBACK, replaces it mid-epoch); GIVEN a record standing in the absorbed body at body-frame (x, y) and a weld with `frame_shift_px` (dx, dy), WHEN the next run executes, THEN `pos_px` = (x + dx, y + dy), `offset_seen_px` equals the new `section_offset(section_id)` and E8 was called once with that point; a cut reverses it *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *Covers HE-22, E-HE-29, E-HE-30.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Modular Ship Assembly is `Status: Designed`; testable.)*
- **AC-HE-48b (auto) The re-based HORROR subject reads the survivor body's light.** *(new 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* GIVEN the record of AC-HE-48 after the weld, WHEN the Emitter's subject table is read at the next tick, THEN the HORROR subject's `body_id` equals the survivor body and its `lux_pm` from a home-side lamp across an open hatch equals the ordinary Emitter D.7 contribution. *Covers HE-22, HE-34.*
- **AC-HE-49 (auto) Speed cadence.** GIVEN `V_HUNT_MPX` 1500 and `acc` 0, WHEN a run executes, THEN `v_eff` per tick is 1, 2, 1, 2, 1 (7 px) and the next run 2, 1, 2, 1, 2 (8 px); at `V_CREEP_MPX` 1000 it is always 1 (5 px); a walking seat and a CRITICAL walk-only seat each outpace the hunting horror; a carrier (`V_CARRY` 1) does not (intended, C-HE-8). The data load refuses any `V_HUNT_MPX >= V_WALK × 1000` (2000) and any `V_CREEP_MPX` below 1000. *Covers HE-24, HE-F6.*
- **AC-HE-50 (auto) Octant stepping.** GIVEN a direction to the next waypoint, WHEN a run executes, THEN one octant is chosen (the integer argmax of the eight unit vectors, ties lowest octant), the horror takes five steps with it, positions and residue persist, and `pos_prev` is the position at run start. *Covers HE-23.*
- **AC-HE-51 (auto) Pathing.** GIVEN a 14-room body, WHEN the path is computed on a cache miss, THEN expansions are at most `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` (14 on the slice, bound 64); a hit costs 0; a door change bumps `version(section_id)` and so changes the cache key; a NONE or UNKNOWN Layout read holds the waypoint for at most `HOLD_MAX_RUNS` 12 and then falls back **by phase: LURK in CALM and in TELL, STALK holding position in BREACH (never LURK; AC-HE-28's "never in LURK during BREACH" holds), RETREAT in SUBSIDING**, and is never read as unlit or as open (a BREACH plus UNKNOWN fixture is run for 40 runs and the state is STALK at every run); zero `probe()` calls; after `BLOCKED_RUNS_MAX` 6 no-movement runs the cached hop is discarded and re-pathed exactly once, and after a second 6 runs the record is wedged and goes to RETREAT with `horror_wedged` incremented. Door state comes from Layout's real `door_passage` (AC-HE-138 (f)); the row never reads NOT ASSESSED for want of the read *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *Covers HE-25, HE-F10, E-HE-31, E-HE-32.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-128 (auto) One pathing graph; L-shaped footprint; passable leaf.** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* Door state comes from Layout's real `door_passage` driven through Layout's `StubOpeningRead` at `area_max` 64 (the 12 px, 16 px and 6 px openings are area 8, 11 and 4, D.5: 12, 16, 6; passable iff `open_extent_px >= 16` across an E-W run, `>= 8` across a N-S run). GIVEN a goal room reachable only through a door in an E-W wall opened to 12 px (below the foot extent `FOOT_BOX_W` 16 across that run), a second fixture with the leaf opened to 16 px, and doors in a N-S wall opened to 12 px (above the foot extent `FOOT_BOX_H` 8: passable) and to 6 px (not), each waypoint being the centre of the hinge-aware `gap_rect`, WHEN the horror paths, THEN the 12 px E-W leaf and the 6 px N-S leaf are excluded (the surrogate goal is the reachable room with the smallest full-adjacency hop distance to the goal, ties lowest room id) and the 16 px E-W leaf and the 12 px N-S leaf are taken; GIVEN an L-shaped room whose straight octant line from the sill to the far anchor crosses a solid corner, WHEN the horror walks it, THEN it reaches the anchor within `ARRIVE_PX` 8 using the slide rule (`blocked_runs` 1 and 2) in at most 40 runs, and never overshoots a waypoint at `V_HUNT_MPX` (8 px per run at most); GIVEN a footprint with no passable route (a console wedge), THEN after two `BLOCKED_RUNS_MAX` windows the state is RETREAT and `horror_wedged` is 1, never a silent permanent stall. *Covers HE-25, HE-F10, E-HE-32.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-137 (auto) A door closed on the horror.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the horror standing with its foot box over a sill, last octant east, WHEN a leaf closes on that sill (Layout's close-safety counted only seats), THEN on the next run guard 4 ejects it along `−last_octant` in 8 px steps to the first position with `solid_in(foot_box)` false (at most 32 px, at most four queries) and `horror_door_ejected` is 1; at no run end is the horror inside a solid; GIVEN no free position within 32 px, THEN it is wedged (RETREAT, `horror_wedged` 1) and not embedded; the fixtures run an E-W and a N-S leaf, closing from each side. *Covers HE-26, E-HE-57.*
- **AC-HE-142 (auto) A closed door is a priced refuge.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the lit seat behind a closed leaf and the horror walking to the surrogate goal, THEN it stops at the sill, lays exactly one HAIRLINES mark on the leaf face on the run it first becomes stationary (`held_at_leaf` true, `horror_leaf_marks` 1), makes no M17 call, no `apply_suit_damage` and begins no REACH, and stays through the BREACH while that seat is the target; GIVEN the seat opening the door, THEN `held_at_leaf` clears and the REACH rules apply; GIVEN the target changing to a reachable lit seat, THEN it leaves the sill and `held_at_leaf` clears. Door state comes from Layout's real `door_passage` (AC-HE-138 (f)); the row never reads NOT ASSESSED for want of the read *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *Covers HE-26, E-HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-52 (auto) A sealed target.** GIVEN the highest-`lux_pm` seat sealed behind a closed leaf, WHEN the horror acts, THEN it stops in the last reachable room at the leaf, begins no REACH, does not switch to a dimmer reachable seat and `apply_suit_damage` count is 0. Door state comes from Layout's real `door_passage` (AC-HE-138 (f)); the row never reads NOT ASSESSED for want of the read *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *Covers E-HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-53 (auto) Counterplay, one fixture each.** GIVEN each of the six MVP responses, WHEN executed, THEN the outcome holds. (a) *Retreat:* a seat walking away at `V_WALK` from a hunting horror gains 500 milli-px per tick and is never inside `R_SNAP_PX` at `reach_end_T`. (b) *Close a door:* no hit through the leaf and no `set_opening` call from Horror. (c) *Go dark:* the next run's target is ANCHOR and `apply_suit_damage` count is 0 that breach. (d) *Pod-hop:* the departed seat leaves `locus_body`, `exogenous_permitted(actor_section(seat))` fails and the in-flight REACH on it fizzles; a home-ship breach, or a co-op seat that is not the last recorded occupant, does not by itself end the breach (AT-12 (iii)). (e) *Drop cargo:* a carrier whose speed becomes `V_WALK` inside 54 ticks clears within `t_out_max` 37 ticks (36 on a cardinal octant); one that does not is caught except on the 11 of 18 draws with `UNFOLD_TICKS >= 130`. (f) *Glowstick work-light:* a Chemlight pool is never a target; a seat standing in it is lit and is a candidate. *Covers HE-26, E-HE-19, E-HE-24, E-HE-27.*
- **AC-HE-54 (auto + review) Dead-end authoring defect.** GIVEN a seat in a dead-end room with a floor span below 144 px and a single exit, WHEN a REACH resolves at P in that room, THEN the seat is struck without a legal walk-out and the case is recorded as an authoring defect, not a Horror rule; the lint is an owed row to Derelict Authoring. *Covers E-HE-26.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the lint exists as `DEAD_END_NARROW` (ERROR, CI), Derelict Authoring `content_lint_ids`; testable.)*

### Group F: footprints and marks (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-55 (auto; smoke) Window budget and saturation.** GIVEN window 5 holds the Horror runs at T = 30 and T = 35, with the run at 30 issuing GOUGE and CHILL (2) and the run at 35 issuing CHILL (1), WHEN windows 0 to 5 execute, THEN `n_5` is 3 and windows 1 to 4 (one run each at T = 10, 15, 20, 25) are within 8; GIVEN a stub requesting 20 deposits in window 5 while Attention also writes 8 STRESS, THEN Horror is granted 4 plus 4, `horror_footprints_withheld` is 12 and the Graph sees 16 and drops none; nominal play of a full breach leaves the counter at 0; `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 5 fails the data load (`2 × 5 > 8`, negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-29, HE-F1, E-HE-45.*
- **AC-HE-56 (auto) Priority under the cap.** GIVEN a window already at 6 accepted calls and a run requesting RUPTURE, GOUGE and CHILL, WHEN it executes, THEN RUPTURE and GOUGE are issued (8), CHILL is withheld, its rime mark is still laid and `horror_footprints_withheld` increments by 1. *Covers E-HE-13.*
- **AC-HE-57 (auto) Mark production.** GIVEN a creep of 480 px and one REACH, WHEN the ring is read, THEN a SMEAR was laid per `MARK_SPACING_PX` 96 of travel plus one touch-down SMEAR per REACH hold stage, a RIME per CHILL deposit (per `CHILL_SPACING_PX` 384 and at each LURK dwell start), HAIRLINES at each STRIKE, GOUGE, RUPTURE, anchor REACH, ingress and egress, one STRUCK per STRIKE on which `apply_suit_damage` was called and none on a miss (AC-HE-121); a stationary horror lays nothing. *Covers HE-27, HE-30.*
- **AC-HE-58 (auto) Per-run mark cap and ids.** GIVEN a run that wants three inserts, WHEN it executes, THEN the first `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` 2 are inserted in the order STRUCK, HAIRLINES, RIME, SMEAR, the rest are counted `horror_marks_withheld`, and `mark_id` is never reused. *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-9.*
- **AC-HE-59 (auto) Ring eviction by quota.** GIVEN a full ring (32) holding 3 hairline marks, 3 STRUCK marks, 7 rime marks and 19 smears, WHEN a new smear is inserted, THEN the oldest smear is evicted and no other kind; WHEN a fourth HAIRLINES mark is inserted, THEN the oldest HAIRLINES mark is evicted and the ring length stays 32; WHEN an eighth rime is inserted, THEN the oldest rime is evicted; a ring without a smear cannot occur, and a fixture that forces it fires the defensive assertion (lowest `mark_id` evicted, `horror_ring_invariant` incremented). *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-8.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-HE-60 (auto) Lifetime and stages.** GIVEN marks in a loaded section, WHEN runs execute, THEN `life_left` falls by 5 per run only for loaded sections; the stage is `min(2, floor(3 × age / MARK_LIFE_TICKS))` (a mark with `life_left` 12000 is stage 1; at 5999 stage 2); every mutation declares explicit dirty keys (a missing key fails the save-equivalence test, AC-HE-76). *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-46.*
- **AC-HE-61 (lint + review) Rime and mark honesty.** GIVEN every Horror mark asset swatch and the generating code, WHEN scanned, THEN no flesh (hue 5-30, S 20-60 %, V 45-85 %), no violet (hue 270-330, S above 25 %), no Alarm Red, Chemlight or Tungsten; rime is hue-less; branch angles are a pure function of the mark's `seed` and two marks with different seeds do not share an identical angle set; no rime within `FITTING_CLEARANCE_PX` 48 of a coolant fitting (the deposit and the mark are both withheld there, `horror_chill_withheld_fitting` increments). The rendered-pixel half (L\* 70 to 78 on the lit floor) is in AC-HE-93. *Covers HE-31.* *The art-director's proposal that no two adjacent branch spacings lie within 5 degrees is not in HE-31; it is carried as an Open Question (C2) and is not asserted here.*
- **AC-HE-62 (auto) CHILL bound.** GIVEN `CHILL_DEPOSITS_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 24 deposits of `CHILL_STEP_DJ` 3000, WHEN summed, THEN the epoch total is 50 mC per deposit and 1200 mC in a 20-tile room, 2000 mC in a 12-tile room and 3000 mC in an 8-tile room (lint: at `n_tiles_min` 8 the total is at most `CHILL_EPOCH_MAX_MC` 5000); a room at −9000 mC deposits (−9050 ≥ −10000) and a room at −9990 mC withholds (−10040 < −10000, `horror_chill_withheld_cold`) with the rime mark still laid; cumulative Horror CHILL never brings a compartment to `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` −15000; `CHILL_STEP_DJ > FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q` fails the load (negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-31, HE-F4.*
- **AC-HE-63 (auto) Filter bit-identity.** GIVEN the same replay with and without a Perception Filter stub that hides the body from one seat, WHEN both run, THEN `state_hash` of `horror.state`, every M17 write, every `apply_suit_damage` call, `horror.view.disturbances[]` and the mark ring are bit-identical; the touch-down smear survives a HIDE entry; Sanity-state fringes never alter marks. *Covers HE-32, E-HE-48, E-HE-51.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the Perception Filter is `Status: Designed` and its truth table row 7 repeats HE-32 (TRUTH_PX marks, body-only HIDE); testable against the Filter's stub.)*
- **AC-HE-64 (auto) Phantoms leave nothing.** GIVEN a phantom horror injected by a Filter stub and co-occurring with a real one, WHEN the replay runs, THEN `horror.state` gains no record or mark, no M17 call is made for it, nothing is registered with the Emitter, TellAudit records nothing for it, and the real one proceeds on its own clock. *Covers HE-32, E-HE-49.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the Filter is designed and its phantom rule (no collision, no light, no footprint, no SIM subject) repeats this; testable.)*
- **AC-HE-121 (auto) The hit mark.** GIVEN two identical replays of one REACH that differ only in whether a seat stands inside `R_SNAP_PX` of P at `reach_end_T`, WHEN each STRIKE entry run completes, THEN the hit replay holds exactly one STRUCK mark at P (one mark even with two seats in the disc) and the miss replay holds none; both hold the touch-down SMEAR laid at the hold stage; GIVEN a REACH that fizzles, THEN no STRUCK mark is laid; GIVEN a seat in the disc but a failed predicate, THEN no suit hit and no STRUCK mark; the STRUCK mark survives a restore bit-identically, is not evicted while any SMEAR is resident, and is laid identically with and without a Perception Filter stub (HE-32). *Covers HE-15, HE-27, HE-30, validation target (1).*

### Group L: light and registration (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-65 (auto) Disturbance eligibility.** GIVEN a horror near a mixed set of lamps (FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN, `fx_cryo_lamp`, a DOCK_STATUS glow within `L3_RADIUS_PX` 144 of the horror, BEAM, BEACON, FAULT_GLOW and a Chemlight DEPLOYABLE) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and the phase forced through CALM, TELL, BREACH and SUBSIDING for 5000 ticks each, WHEN runs execute, THEN disturbances start in every phase and only on FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN within `L3_RADIUS_PX` 144 of the horror; never on the EMERGENCY subkind or the DOCK_STATUS subkind (the DOCK_STATUS glow is never selected for a disturbance across the runs, so it never consumes an `L3_MAX_ACTIVE` 4 slot, never enters the `last_end_T` map and never starts an `L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS` gap); `strength_pm` is at most `1000 − MOD_FLOOR_PM`; `dur_ticks` is in [`L3_DUR_LO` 18, `L3_DUR_HI` 30]; the gap per lamp is at least `L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 120; at most `L3_MAX_ACTIVE` 4 are active; the `last_end_T` map never exceeds `L3_MAX_TRACKED` 16; the disturbance list is persisted; the (`strength_pm`, `dur_ticks`) pair of each disturbance equals the value computed from `he_hash` (the golden inputs of AC-HE-139; a deterministic function, so no probabilistic distinctness is asserted); Horror never writes an Emitter field or calls Lighting. *Covers HE-35, HE-F11, C-HE-29.*
- **AC-HE-66 (auto) L3 arithmetic.** GIVEN `MOD_FLOOR_PM` 600, WHEN hash h2 = 977, THEN `strength_pm` = 150 + (977 mod 251) = 374; GIVEN `MOD_FLOOR_PM` changed to 800, THEN `S_hi` re-derives to 200 and `L3_STRENGTH_LO_PM` above it fails the load; no result is below `MOD_FLOOR_PM` after Lighting's `modifier_stack`. *Covers HE-F11.*
- **AC-HE-134 (auto) L3 sign and the post-chain output.** GIVEN `MOD_FLOOR_PM` 600, a lamp with `M1 = 1000`, `M2 = 1000` and a published `strength_pm` 374, WHEN Lighting's chain runs, THEN `M3 = 626` and `m_mod = 626`; GIVEN `M1 = 700`, THEN the product `floor(700 × 1000 × 626 / 10^6) = 438` is clamped and `m_mod = 600`; GIVEN reduced motion on and `strength_pm` 374, THEN Lighting reads `min(374, L3_STRENGTH_RM_CAP_PM 150) = 150`, `M3 = 850` and `m_mod = 850` (with `M1 = 1000`); GIVEN reduced motion on and `strength_pm` 150 (the range floor, equal to the cap), THEN `M3 = 850` (`strength_pm` 120 lies outside `[L3_STRENGTH_LO_PM, S_hi]` and is not a fixture value); in every case the post-chain value is at least `MOD_FLOOR_PM` and the SIM `strength_pm` and the hashed state are unchanged by the setting. *Covers HE-F11, Accessibility.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-67 (auto, reruns EM AC-24 and AC-LO-16) No occluder, no emission.** GIVEN a lit scene with and without a real horror between an emitter and a subject, WHEN compared, THEN `lux`, `lit`, `lit_by`, the occluder set, the shadow render and `emitters()` are identical. *Covers HE-2.*
- **AC-HE-68 (auto) Registration.** GIVEN boot and ingress, WHEN the record first registers, THEN E6 returns true, E8 runs once per run and E7 at egress or unload; GIVEN a restore, THEN the Emitter registration set equals the pre-save set (EM AC-42), any `post_restore` E6 on an already-registered ref returns false and is not counted as a defect, and `state_hash` is unchanged; `S_h <= HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2; `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX + A_max <= MAX_MOVING_SUBJECTS` (2 + 4 ≤ 16); a stub forcing a first-registration E6 false (negative fixture, expected FAIL) fails the build as a budget defect while the horror still moves and strikes. *Covers HE-34, HE-F12, E-HE-44.*
- **AC-HE-140 (auto) Registration on body load.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN derelict B unloaded with a resident LURK (registered when last loaded, unregistered at unload), WHEN B loads and the first Horror run executes, THEN E6 is called once at the record's stored `pos_px` before any other act in that run and `lux(ref)` is readable in the same run (first boarding finds it lit by the player's lamp); GIVEN a restore into a loaded body with the ref absent from the Emitter table, THEN `post_restore` and the first run together call E6 once; GIVEN a TRANSIT record, THEN no E6; GIVEN the body unloading with the ref registered, THEN E7 is called once and no other act is performed. *Covers HE-34, guard 3.*
- **AC-HE-69 (auto + lint) No noise push.** GIVEN a full breach cycle, WHEN `push_stimulus` call counts are read, THEN Horror made zero calls (AC-AT-95 refuses a Horror double as a caller; Horror is not a declared caller and makes zero calls). *Covers HE-36.*

### Group I: information, rules and reward (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-70 (auto) The published fields.** GIVEN each state, WHEN `horror.view` is read, THEN it publishes always `entity_id`, `type_id`, `state`, `body_id`, `section_id`, `pos_px`; LURK, STALK and RETREAT add `state_enter_T`, the disturbance list and the counters; REACH adds `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T`; STRIKE adds `strike_T`, the seats hit, the hull segments gouged or ruptured and the `tell_epoch` it ran in; TRANSIT adds `transit_until_T` and the pending ingress segment and has no `pos_px`; `type_id` and `tell_id` enumerations are stable across a version bump. *Covers HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-71 (auto + lint) Audio family disjointness.** GIVEN Adaptive Audio's master identity table (AA-6) exported as a list of `(family, cue_id, bus, asset_tag)` rows, WHEN the test intersects the set of cue ids and asset tags in Horror's families with the sets of Attention's tell family, the Graph loop sounds and Sanity's hallucination family, THEN every intersection has size 0 and each Horror cue id appears in exactly one family; the set of facts Audio reads from `horror.view` (`state`, `pos_px`, `section_id`, `locked_pt`, `reach_end_T`, `strike_T`, ingress and egress events) is a subset of the published HE-37 list; a scan of the Horror source finds 0 calls to any mute, duck, filter or bus-volume API; and a phantom-horror fixture routed through the Filter plays only cue ids tagged to the Filter or Sanity families (0 Graph witness classes). *Covers HE-37.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Adaptive Audio is `Status: Designed`; was a review row with no pass threshold.)*
- **AC-HE-72 (lint + auto) No combat, no reward.** GIVEN the data files and the repository, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no field named `damage`, `accuracy`, `armour`, `dps`, `xp`, `unlock`, `health`, `hp`, `kill`, `loot`, `threat`, `aggro` or `hate`; no hit flash, damage number, health pip, threat bar, proximity ring or shake call from a Horror source; every Interaction verb, iterated by name from the Interaction data, forced at a HORROR subject returns `INVALID_TARGET`; nothing drops, unlocks or awards. *Covers HE-5, HE-38, E-HE-28.*
- **AC-HE-73 (review) Suppressant.** GIVEN `tool_suppressant` and `repel(origin, T)`, WHEN reviewed, THEN no effect ships at MVP; every `repel` criterion (RETREAT for `REPEL_TICKS` 600 within `REPEL_RADIUS_PX` 192, no REACH or STRIKE in the window, never dies, drops nothing, never touches Attention) reads **NOT ASSESSED**, never PASS; C-HE-6 is closed by the user ruling (Option A, X6; Open Question 3; gate AC-HE-124, BLOCKING); the counterplay set of HE-26 contains no tool. *Covers HE-39, HE-26.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HE-74 (auto + lint) Rig presenter contract.** GIVEN the rig presenter, WHEN driven by a Horror ref, THEN `leak` is 0; a rig with no Horror ref and `leak` 0 is a lint error (negative fixture, expected FAIL); the pose changes only on ticks that are multiples of 5; segment rotation is a multiple of 15 degrees; Horror never writes the camera. *Covers HE-40, HE-F18.* The violet and flesh pixel scan is in AC-HE-93 and the camera-jolt refusal of a Horror-tagged caller is the Pipeline's own API refusal (`diag.jolt_refused = [caller]`, PIPE jolt rule), asserted here by a stub caller tagged Horror *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the Pipeline is designed)*.
- **AC-HE-75 (lint) One type row and the data contract.** GIVEN the data and the boot check, WHEN a second type row or `HORROR_MAX_MVP` 2 is configured, THEN the data lint and the boot check refuse it; the behaviour code contains no numeric literal for any row field; every field of the HE-5 row is present. *Covers HE-1, HE-5, E-HE-54, R26.*

### Group S: state, restore and determinism (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-76 (auto) Block conformance.** GIVEN `horror.state`, WHEN conformance tests run, THEN class SIM, `persist` true, `persist_scope` WORLD, scope SHARED, `repl_hint` SHARED_SUMMARY, `schema_version` 1, `supported_versions` [1], `restore_after` `[layout.state, graph.state, emitter.state, attention.state]`; plain data only (no `StringName` keys, no `Vector2i` values); **the snapshot's key set equals the committed manifest** `tests/unit/horror_entities/horror_state_manifest.json` (one row per HE-41 field: name, type, range, group; no key beyond the manifest and none missing; it includes the group-1 int2 `offset_seen_px` of HE-22 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*), which is reviewed against the HE-41 text and never generated from the implementation; **a mutation test**: for each manifest field a fixture snapshot is perturbed to a different in-range value, restored and run for 600 ticks from a fixture in which that field is read, and the run's `state_hash` must differ from the unperturbed continuation (a field that changes nothing is dead state and a defect; the fixture per field is part of the manifest); **a lint** of the `horror.step` source finds no instance field that is read in a later run, is not in the manifest and is not on the non-persisted whitelist (the target, the path cache, `horror.view.debug`); the snapshot contains neither the target nor the path cache; `snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` is byte-identical; the ring never exceeds `MAX_MARKS` 32 and no quota'd kind exceeds its quota; `gouged_seg_ids` never exceeds `GOUGES_PER_EPOCH_MAX`. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-46.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier row checked "every field of HE-41 is present", which tested the implementation against its own list and let the gaps pass review twice)*
- **AC-HE-77 (auto) The six restore codes, one crafted save each.** GIVEN `HORROR_RESTORE_SCHEMA` (schema_version 2), `_RANGE` (`acc_mpx` 1000, `strikes_epoch` above `STRIKES_EPOCH_MAX` 13, and one crafted save per new field of HE-41: `goal_commit_runs`, `smear_travel_px`, `chill_travel_px`, `gouged_seg_ids`, `seats_hit`, `exo_source_class`, `transit_leg`, `dwell_start_T`, `ingress_mark_T`), `_STATE_INCOHERENT` (a TRANSIT record with a registered subject, or a `transit_leg` set outside TRANSIT), `_SECTION_UNKNOWN` (a position in a section id Layout does not know), `_MARK_RING` (ring length 33) and `_RNG_COUNTER` (a `"horror.tell"` counter that is negative, non-integer or at or above `RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE`), WHEN each is restored, THEN it fails hard with the matching code and the Save/Load layer refuses the file; nothing is clamped. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-40, SL-15.* *The `RNG_COUNTER` trigger is now defined in HE-41 (C-HE-30 closed, Open Question 10).* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **AC-HE-78 (auto) Restore into REACH.** GIVEN a save taken in REACH with the predicate true, WHEN restored, THEN `reach_end_T`, `locked_pt` and the `"horror.tell"` counter are unchanged (the remaining telegraph is exactly `reach_end_T − T_restore`, never shortened or lengthened, no reroll) and the run that follows matches the uninterrupted run; GIVEN the predicate false at restore, THEN RETREAT with no strike, no hairlines and no GOUGE or RUPTURE. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-36, E-HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-79 (auto) Restore into STRIKE and TRANSIT.** GIVEN saves in STRIKE and in TRANSIT with `transit_until_T` in the past, WHEN restored, THEN no second `apply_suit_damage` or M17 call is made for the STRIKE and the TRANSIT record arrives at the next legal run. *Covers E-HE-38, E-HE-39.*
- **AC-HE-80 (auto) Determinism.** GIVEN two runs with the same `world_seed`, the same (save, `restore_epoch`) pair and inputs, WHEN 36000 ticks complete, THEN `state_hash` matches every 600 ticks and at every state change; the only random source is `rng_derive_epoch(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)`; mark seeds, LURK waypoints and L3 timing are `he_hash` values; a lint finds no wall clock and no global RNG. *Covers HE-42, E-HE-47.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: kernel name `rng_derive_epoch`)*
- **AC-HE-81 (auto) Save-equivalence at named saves.** GIVEN block-level snapshots (not Save/Load autosaves, since SL-5 defers an autosave in a told breach) taken for each of LURK, STALK, REACH, STRIKE, RETREAT and TRANSIT at state entry, entry plus one run, the midpoint and the last run, WHEN each is restored and run for 1800 ticks, THEN `state_hash`, the mark ring and every footprint equal the uninterrupted run given the same (save, `restore_epoch`) pair (G4; a restore that advances the epoch changes only the lengths of REACHes not yet begun, AC-HE-136) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **Named mid-state saves** (each is also a perturbation target of the mutation test in AC-HE-76): (a) between a GOUGE and the strike that would RUPTURE it (`gouged_seg_ids` non-empty); (b) between two smears and between two CHILL deposits (`smear_travel_px` and `chill_travel_px` mid-interval); (c) mid-dwell in LURK (`dwell_start_T`); (d) mid-walk in LURK with `goal_wp` set; (e) in TRANSIT between the ingress mark (`ingress_mark_T` passed) and the arrival, for an OUTBOUND and for a RETURN leg; (f) in STALK mid-commit (`goal_commit_runs` > 0); (g) in REACH just before and just after `hold_start_T` (`reach_smear_laid` false, then true); (h) in STALK at a closed leaf (`held_at_leaf` true). *Covers HE-41.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HE-82 (auto) A death restarts from the autosave.** GIVEN the player dies at a third strike, WHEN the game restarts from the newest autosave, THEN `horror.state` restores with the WORLD blocks including the `"horror.tell"` counter (unchanged, continuing from its saved value), the marks, positions and footprints equal the uninterrupted run, and the `restore_epoch` read by the next REACH through `rng_derive_epoch` is the restore's (the death stub advances it by 1, durably, before requesting the restore, R13/R14; a stub supplies the advanced epoch per R13) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14)*, so the first `UNFOLD_TICKS` after the restore equals the stream value at (new epoch, saved counter) and is not asserted equal to the pre-death draw; any snapshot taken in CALM has `state` in {LURK, RETREAT, TRANSIT} and never REACH or STRIKE. *Covers HE-10, HE-42, E-HE-41, E-HE-42.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-HE-136 (auto) The REACH-length draw keys on `restore_epoch` through the kernel's one derivation.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R1)* GIVEN `world_seed` 0x5EED000000002026 and `restore_epoch` pinned at 0, 1 and 2, WHEN the REACH-length draw is taken, THEN `UNFOLD_TICKS` at counter 17 is 155, 130 and 150 and at counter 0 is 120, 115 and 165 (golden values of `rng_derive(seed, "horror.tell", counter + 2^20 × restore_epoch)` and `rng_int(19, 36)`; the epoch-0 values rest on the kernel's D.10 vectors `0xB2AA94991B52A02E` and `0x4988DBEFD7F20FF5`); every value is a multiple of 5 in [`UNFOLD_LO` 95, `UNFOLD_HI` 180]; counters 0..17 give, at epoch 0, 120, 135, 105, 180, 165, 95, 110, 120, 170, 145, 140, 150, 115, 175, 175, 125, 175, 155 and, at epoch 1, 115, 130, 100, 180, 110, 130, 115, 165, 170, 110, 140, 130, 100, 160, 100, 155, 130, 130. **The four required restore cases (R1 as amended by R13/R14)** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14)*: (i) a death-stub restore yields e + 1 and a different REACH draw at the same counter; (ii) a Save & Quit then a (fresh-process) Continue of the newest `QUIT` generation keeps e and replays the identical draw; (iii) a process kill, then a fresh-process Continue of an `AUTO` or `EVENT` generation, yields e + 1 (the value is read from Save/Load's `slot_epoch.bin`, never held in process memory) (asserted by KRN-EPO-04 (iii) and AC-SL-145 (iii)); (iv) the same (save, epoch) pair replays identically (G4); these are the same four cases as the kernel's KRN-EPO-04 and Save/Load's AC-SL-145. GIVEN a save in REACH restored with the epoch advanced, THEN `reach_end_T`, `locked_pt` and the counter are unchanged (the remaining telegraph is exactly `reach_end_T − T_restore`); GIVEN a save in LURK restored with the epoch advanced and run to its next REACH, THEN the counter continues from the saved value (it is not reset) and `UNFOLD_TICKS` equals the draw at (new epoch, saved counter); mark seeds, LURK waypoints and L3 timing are identical across epochs (`he_hash` reads no epoch); a lint finds `restore_epoch` read only through `rng_derive_epoch` at the REACH draw and never stored in `horror.state`, and the lint `counter < RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE` holds. The kernel has published both surfaces this row needs: the read view `kernel.clock.restore_epoch` (KRN CR-8) and the helper `rng_derive_epoch(world_seed, stream_name, rng_counter)` with `counter_eff = rng_counter + RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE × restore_epoch` (KRN D.10; KRN-EPO-01 to 03). **This row is assessed on the Horror side and reads PASS or FAIL, never NOT ASSESSED:** the harness calls the kernel directly with a pinned epoch (a stub `request_restore(payload, restore_epoch)` supplying e, then e + 1, then e again, exactly as KRN-EPO-01 does) and asserts the Horror half of cases (i) to (iv): (i) epoch e then e + 1 gives a different REACH draw at the same counter (counter 17: 155 then 130; counter 0: 120 then 115); (ii) and (iv) the same (save, epoch) pair replays the identical draw, REACH, positions, marks and `state_hash`; (iii) a restore supplied with e + 1 draws the golden value at (e + 1, saved counter). The on-disk halves (the `slot_epoch.bin` staging step, `EPOCH_DURABLE`, the QUIT carry, the kill-then-Continue advance, the failure path) are asserted by KRN-EPO-04 and KRN-EPO-05 and by Save/Load AC-SL-142 and AC-SL-145 in the Save/Load restore story; this row cites them and does not carry them as preconditions (the increment-and-durable-write rule is Save/Load's criterion and the derivation `rng_derive_epoch` is the kernel's; both are cited, not asserted). A failure in those rows blocks their own story, not this one. *Covers HE-42, E-HE-41.* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: name conformed to the kernel's `rng_derive_epoch`; row assessable on the Horror side, delegated ruling R1 as amended by R13/R14)*
- **AC-HE-139 (auto) `he_hash` golden vectors and the integer order of operations.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN `HE_HASH_SEED` 0x4845 and the kernel's pinned `mix64`, `lsr` and `GOLDEN`, WHEN `he_hash` is evaluated, THEN `he_hash(1, 0, 0, SALT_MARK)` = `0x2B1792AE18418A3E`, `he_hash(7, 3, 0, SALT_WP)` = `0x42973A88C2EF0EA3`, `he_hash(12, 40, 0, SALT_L3_START)` = `0x7AB270A9751F8EAA`, `he_hash(12, 40, 0, SALT_L3_STRENGTH)` = `0x2A017D540A5BCB1A` and `he_hash(12, 40, 0, SALT_L3_DUR)` = `0x77219910C6F9EE48`; every result is non-negative (bit 63 clear); with `L3_START_PM` 40 the start test on the third value (`mod 1000` = 306) fails; with `S_hi` 400 and `L3_STRENGTH_LO_PM` 150 the strength from the fourth is `150 + (… mod 251)` = 298, and with `L3_DUR_LO` 18 the duration from the fifth is `18 + (… mod 13)` = 22; `UNFOLD_TICKS` for draw 21 at scale 105 is 115 and at scale 100 is 105; changing `HE_HASH_SEED` changes all five values. *Covers HE-42, HE-F5, HE-F11.*
- **AC-HE-83 (auto, exhaustive) The phase by state matrix.** GIVEN the 4 phases by 6 states table of States and Transitions, WHEN each of the 24 cells is driven by a fixture, THEN the cell holds (resident, to-LURK, fizzle, cannot begin, hunt, harm on predicate, cooldown, arrives-as); AND for each of the seven ordered guards one precedence fixture holds, including restore-first beating a phase edge on the same run, the tell edge (guard 2) beating dormancy (guard 3) for a record whose body is unloaded, and a layout change preceding the phase edge *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: six became seven guards)*; and, independent of phase *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, a REACH begun in BREACH resolving in SUBSIDING and fizzling in CALM with no BREACH-only guard involved, a STRIKE ending its hold in any phase, and a new `tell_epoch` seen mid-REACH fizzling it to RETREAT in the same run. *Covers the phase view and the transition guards.*

### Group Q: formula criteria (BLOCKING; values inlined)

- **AC-HE-84 (auto) HE-F1.** The worked window example of AC-HE-55 and the priority RUPTURE, GOUGE, CHILL under the cap of AC-HE-56 hold; `2 × MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN <= HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET` 8.
- **AC-HE-85 (auto) HE-F2.** Boundary dx 71, 72, 73 as in AC-HE-27; a walker from dx 0 east at 2 px per tick is hit at 35 ticks (`dx = 70`, 4900 < 5184) and clear at 36 (`5184 < 5184` false); a runner is clear at 24 and a carrier at 72; integer comparison only (no float, no `sqrt`).
- **AC-HE-86 (auto, parameter sweep) HE-F3.** For every `STRIKE_BP` in 3001..3500, `k_fail(10000) = 3` and `I_2 >= SUIT_HP_FAIL`; for 3000 and 3501 the data load fails (negative fixtures, expected FAIL); `k_fail(7000) = 2`; `k_fail(SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP 6000) = 2` (a patched suit: 2800 then 0). **Corner sweep** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: over the pairs (`SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP`, `STRIKE_BP`) in {5100, 5699, 5700, 6000, 6900} × {3001, 3200, 3500, 3501}, the load accepts exactly the pairs with `cap − STRIKE_BP >= 2500` and `STRIKE_BP <= 3500` ((5700, 3200) and (6000, 3500) pass; (5699, 3200), (5100, 3001) and (6000, 3501) are rejected), and for every accepted pair a patched suit survives one strike and dies at the second.
- **AC-HE-87 (auto) HE-F4.** Worked values 50, 1200, 2000 and 3000 mC; thresholds −9050 passes and −10040 fails; as AC-HE-62.
- **AC-HE-88 (auto) HE-F5.** `UNFOLD_LO` 95 passes; 90 fails the bound (negative fixture, expected FAIL); 91 to 94 fail the multiple-of-5 lint; *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: recomputed under HE-F5's tick-indexing convention, steps = U − a, G4)* a walker whose walk command is issued at tick 54 is clear at U = 90 on a cardinal octant and U = 91 on a diagonal one (the slowest, `t_out_max` 37) and never hit at any of the 18 legal draws; a walker whose command is 5 ticks late (a = 59) is clear at U = 95 (cardinal) or 96 (diagonal), so it is never hit on a cardinal octant and is hit only on the draw of 95 on a diagonal octant (36 steps, 5000 < 5184); a seat staggered at REACH start with its walk command at tick 54 clears at 90 (91 diagonal); the exact slack at U = 95, a = 54 is 5 ticks cardinal / 4 diagonal, and the 4 (cardinal) / 3 (diagonal) figures quoted in HE-F5's Output Range, the Game Feel line and Open Question 17 are the conservative figure that also spends the formula's `+ 1` margin; `INPUT_LATENCY_TICKS` 3 passes the bound (`37 + 54 + 3 + 1 = 95`) and 4 fails it for `UNFOLD_LO` 95 (negative fixture, expected FAIL: it requires `UNFOLD_LO` 100); `UNFOLD_SCALE_PCT` 150 turns every draw `n` (in 5-tick steps) into `5 × ceil_div(n × 150, 100)` ticks and never into a shorter telegraph *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, and 99 fails the load (negative fixture, expected FAIL); the eight-octant fixture is AC-HE-130; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* a seat staggered mid-walk at tick 60 has the 30 frozen ticks counted, and the fixture asserts the resulting hit/clear outcome per draw rather than a clearance; carrier outcomes per draw match 11 of 18 (61.1 %) clear at `UNFOLD_TICKS >= 130`.
- **AC-HE-130 (auto, BLOCKING) Walk-out in all 8 octants.** GIVEN Body's real `LocomotionStep` with `sub` reset to 0, `R_SNAP_PX` 72 and a seat standing on P, WHEN a walk is issued in each of the 8 octants at `V_WALK` 2, `V_RUN` 3 and `V_CARRY` 1, THEN the first tick at which `dx² + dy² >= 5184` is 36, 36, 36, 36 on the four cardinal octants and 37, 37, 37, 37 on the four diagonal octants at `V_WALK`; 24 and 25 at `V_RUN`; 72 and 73 at `V_CARRY`; the table equals the one in HE-F2, and the fixture fails the build if Body's cadence changes it (so a Body retune re-derives HE-F5). A seat that issues its walk at tick 54 (+1 application) is clear at tick 90 (cardinal) or 91 (diagonal) and is never hit at any of the 18 legal draws at any octant; a seat whose walk is issued at a = 59 has taken 36 steps at U 95 and is clear on every cardinal octant and hit on every diagonal octant, and clear on all 8 from U 100 (41 steps) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: one convention with AC-HE-88)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **The row also pins the tick-indexing convention of HE-F5:** with the command issued during tick `R + 54` the seat has taken `U − 54` steps when the strike resolves on the run at `R + U` (41 at `U` 95), read after that tick's `body.integrate`; a kernel that applied commands later, or ran `horror.step` before `body.integrate`, fails this row. *Covers HE-F2, HE-F5, C-HE-22.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-131 (spike) Input and display latency.** GIVEN the release-export build (Godot 4.7.2 release template, `forward_plus`, vsync on, 120-frame warm-up; delegated ruling R12) on each reference machine, RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK, with a hardware input timestamp (a key press or stick deflection) and a display capture (a high-speed camera or a photodiode on the seat's first moved pixel), WHEN 200 walk commands are measured, THEN the P95 latency from input to first displayed motion is recorded in ticks; it passes if it is at most `INPUT_LATENCY_TICKS` (0 plus the 3-tick allowance, 50 ms) above the kernel's one-tick command application; a measured value of 4 ticks or more is a BLOCKING finding that raises the HE-F5 bound to 100 and sets `UNFOLD_LO` 100 with every re-derivation listed in HE-F5. Pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK (figures from a developer machine are indicative only). *Covers HE-F5, Open Question 17.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*
- **AC-HE-132 (auto, integration with Interaction and the Controller) Drop the cargo, then clear the disc.** GIVEN a carrying seat at `V_CARRY`, a REACH whose locked P is its foot point, `UNFOLD_TICKS` at each of its 18 values, and a free cell for the drop, WHEN the seat issues `drop` and a walk command at tick 54 of the telegraph (first step in tick 55), THEN it is outside the disc by tick 91 (diagonal) or 90 (cardinal) and is never hit, with the Controller restoring `V_WALK` on the tick `clear_carry_ref` lands (a one-tick lag still clears by 92) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: HE-F5 tick-indexing convention, G4)*; GIVEN the same seat with no free cell around it, THEN `drop` returns `OBSTRUCTED` and the seat is struck on the 7 of 18 draws below 130, and the fixture records that case as an authoring defect; GIVEN no drop at all, THEN the strike hits on exactly 7 of 18 draws (38.9 %). *Covers HE-24, E-HE-27, C-HE-8, IA-28.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-89 (auto) HE-F6, HE-F14, HE-F10, HE-F12.** Speed tables for 1000 and 1500 as in AC-HE-49; `V_HUNT_MPX` 2000 fails; `ceil(V_MPX / 1000) <= 3`; `72 < 96 < 192` and `96 <= 3 × 53 = 159` (a load with `REACH_RANGE_PX` 160 or 72 fails); expansions at most 14 on the slice; `S_h` never exceeds 2. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Dependent bounds, each a data-load assertion with a negative fixture (expected FAIL):** `R_SNAP_PX < REACH_RANGE_PX < LURK_STANDOFF_PX` (the corners `R_SNAP_PX` 96 with `REACH_RANGE_PX` 73, and `REACH_RANGE_PX` 150 with `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` 144, are rejected); `ARRIVE_PX >= ceil(5 × V_HUNT_MPX / 1000)` (`V_HUNT_MPX` 1999 with `ARRIVE_PX` 8 is rejected); `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS + STALK_VISIBLE_FLOOR_TICKS + INGRESS_MARK_LEAD_TICKS <= TELL_MIN_TICKS` (360 + 360 + 120 is rejected, 300 + 240 + 60 passes with no slack); `MARK_LIFE_TICKS >= smear_retention_ticks`; `HORROR_MARK_DRAW_GROUPS <= D_marks − TRAIL_DMARKS_SHARE`; the L3 flash rate `L3_MAX_ACTIVE × 60 <= 3 × (L3_DUR_LO + L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS)` (`L3_MAX_ACTIVE` 8 with `L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 60 is rejected, 4 with 90 passes at 2.2 per second); `SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP − STRIKE_BP >= SUIT_HP_FAIL` (cap 5100 with `STRIKE_BP` 3200, and cap 6000 with `STRIKE_BP` 3501, are rejected).
- **AC-HE-90 (auto) HE-F7, HE-F9.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **The relation is an arithmetic assertion at data load, not a runtime-derived fixture:** with `MAX_MARKS` 32, quotas 7 / 3 / 3 (`n_other_max` 13), `V_CREEP_MPX` 1000 and `MARK_SPACING_PX` 96, `smear_retention_ticks` is 1824, at or above `TRAIL_TARGET_TICKS` 1800 (pass); the assertion `MAX_MARKS − n_other_max >= 19` rejects quotas summing to 14 at `MAX_MARKS` 32 (13 + 19 = 32 passes, 14 + 19 = 33 fails) and `MARK_LIFE_TICKS` 1800 below the 1824 retention (negative fixtures, expected FAIL, each a load-time rejection of a static value); at `V_HUNT_MPX` 1500 the retention is 1216, recorded as the accepted hunt-speed shortfall and not asserted as a pass. **Two replays count the real ring:** an 18000-tick CALM LURK (dwell starts and creep) never holds more than 7 RIME, and a 3600-tick breach (ingress, creep, up to 8 REACHes, strikes, egress) never holds more than 3 HAIRLINES, 3 STRUCK or 13 non-smear marks in all; every insert at a quota evicts the oldest of its own kind, a SMEAR is resident in every full ring, and the ring never exceeds 32. The ingress timeline of AC-HE-42: `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` 400 fails the three-knob relation, `D_entry_max` is 492.
- **AC-HE-91 (auto) HE-F8, HE-F13, HE-F15, HE-F17, HE-F18.** The five target cases (A lit 1000 against B dark 0 selects A; A lamp off selects ANCHOR; a Chemlight pool reading 700 is never a target while a seat standing in it is a candidate; two lit at 1000 and 1000 select the lower seat id; none lit selects ANCHOR); for `tell_len` 600 the first strike is no earlier than 760 ticks after TELL entry and `layered_warning_min >= W_MIN_WARNING_S × TICK_RATE` (600), and for `tell_len` 1800 no later than 2045, at the pinned `t_arrive` 65 (the range 0..70 is AC-HE-144); a stationary seat's strike-to-strike interval is `UNFOLD_TICKS + 365` (460 to 545 ticks, `min_gap` 395 after a fizzle, `t_out` 96 ≤ `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS`); `alpha5` is 0.5 at two ticks after the run with `tick_alpha` 0.5, and the rig pose never changes between runs.
- **AC-HE-144 (auto) `t_arrive` as a range.** *(new 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN BREACH entry with the seat adjacent (the horror already within `REACH_RANGE_PX`), WHEN the first REACH begins, THEN `t_arrive` is 0 and the first strike lands between `tell_len + 95` and `tell_len + 180` ticks after TELL entry; GIVEN the seat at 192 px on a cardinal axis, THEN `t_arrive` is 65; GIVEN the seat at 192 px at a bearing 22.5° off an octant axis, THEN `t_arrive` is at most 70; in each case `layered_warning_min >= 600`. *Covers HE-F13, HE-F16.*
- **AC-HE-92 (auto, exact enumeration) HE-F16.** GIVEN a stub `rng_int` and all 18 × 18 = 324 pairs of `UNFOLD_TICKS` draws for the first two REACHes of a stationary lit seat at the pinned `t_arrive` 65 and a fresh suit, `I_0 >= 8900` (`s_3 = 795 + U_1 + U_2`; a second enumeration for a worn or patched suit asserts that `T_2` runs 620..790 and that the second strike resolves before B = 900 in 324 of 324 pairs, HE-F16), WHEN the share of pairs with `s_3 < B` is counted, THEN it is 0 of 324 at B = 900, 1 of 324 at B = 990 (0.3 %), 6 of 324 at 1000 (1.9 %), 91 of 324 at 1050 (28.1 %), 246 of 324 at 1100 (75.9 %), 323 of 324 at 1155 (99.7 %) and 324 of 324 at B >= 1156; the mean `T_3` is exactly 1207.5 ticks (range 1080 to 1335). A 1000-seed Monte Carlo is kept only as a **(spike)**, never a pass/fail rule (a ±0.03 tolerance would false-fail about 3 % of runs).

### Group X: cross-system criteria (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-93 (auto, integration) Attention's must-repeat list, item by item.** GIVEN Attention's seven must-repeat items, WHEN each is checked, THEN each is answered: the hook is `after: [attention.step, emitter.place]` (AC-HE-10); no exogenous harm outside BREACH or SUBSIDING in the locus body, asserted through `tell_window_contract` with CHILL excluded (AC-HE-1 to 4, 7); no lethal act on a crew body outside it (AC-HE-5); it honours `TellAudit` (AC-HE-4); the per-window budget (AC-HE-55); the single MVP behaviour is wired to BREACH (**BLOCKING**, AC-HE-12) and a non-lethal presence outside BREACH (**BLOCKING**, AC-HE-14); the suppressant per AT-19 (AC-HE-73); L3 as state (AC-HE-65). *Covers AC-AT-77.*
- **AC-HE-94 (auto, integration) Graph.** GIVEN a GOUGE, WHEN the Graph runs, THEN the segment is `fatigued` and its creep sits under the Graph's own `W_MIN_WARNING_S` gate (Horror neither alters nor shortens it); GIVEN a RUPTURE on a gouged segment, THEN the Graph sets that segment to `RUPTURE_HP` 3600 (hole `A` 7) and raises its own told consequence, a hull descriptor and the decompression clock of 1309 Graph steps (about 131 s) at the initial hole (LSG D.15 BREACH-EVENT, LSG:707, cited from the Graph, never restated as Horror's), and Horror's call is a Class B `deposit_trace` from `horror.step` made in the same Graph window as no more than `HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET` 8 calls and never a `SYSTEM` command. *Covers HE-16, HE-28.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the RUPTURE outcome is cited from LSG:707, so the row is testable.)*
- **AC-HE-95 (auto, integration) Suit.** GIVEN `apply_suit_damage`, WHEN called, THEN it is the 3-argument form; the class travels on `source_ref`, classed EXOGENOUS once at record creation (boot or ingress arrival), persisted with the record, and valid across restore independently of E6; an unclassed ref is a programmer error that fails the build; Horror reads no integrity value and no Suit rung. *Covers HE-15, AC-SE-41.*
- **AC-HE-96 (lint + manual) Failure & Trail.** GIVEN the sprite sheets of Failure & Trail's RUPTURE trail descriptor and of Horror's hairline mark, WHEN a pixel scan runs, THEN the hairline sheet has 0 transparent pixels within 1 px of its centre cell (no hole), 0 strokes narrower than 2 px, and its stroke directions cover at least 6 of 8 octants around the centre (radial), while the F&T descriptor's strokes are collinear within 15 degrees of the rib or seam axis; and in a blind trial of N ≥ 10 viewers each shown 10 frames (5 hairline, 5 trail) at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2`, at least 90 % of labels are correct (the shared target of AC-HE-110); a scan finds 0 Graph descriptor drawn by a Horror source and 0 Horror mark drawn by a F&T source. *Covers FT-31.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was "never confused" with no threshold)*
- **AC-HE-97 (review) Owed quotes.** GIVEN Interaction and Derelict Authoring, WHEN reviewed, THEN each quotes `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2 and not 12 or 16 (C-HE-4, closed 2026-10-27), and IA-31 quotes `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 (C-HE-5, closed 2026-10-27).
- **AC-HE-98 (lint + auto) Body.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no `AGENT` row for a horror, no `CharacterBody2D`, no physics query and no `NavigationServer2D`; movement is only through `LocomotionStep` with the foot box `FOOT_BOX_W` 16 by `FOOT_BOX_H` 8; a fixture in which a seat's body overlaps the 64 to 96 px sprite extent but not the foot box shows the seat neither blocked nor struck; the reads are `actors_on`, `pos_curr`, `actor_section` and `loco_state`, none per tick. *Covers HE-23, HE-33.*
- **AC-HE-99 (auto) Layout reads.** GIVEN a run, WHEN reads are counted, THEN `room_adjacency` is read on a cache miss only, `room_at`, `anchor_geometry` and `solid_in` (at most 5) per run, `body_of` and `sections_of`; none per tick; `hull_segments_near`, `version(section_id)` and `emitters_near` are each read at most once per run and per cache miss, and a run with the lookup stubbed out fails the criteria that need it. *Covers HE-25, HE-F10.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the reads are published, C-HE-11, C-HE-13 and C-HE-15 closed; testable.)*
- **AC-HE-100 (spike + manual) Pipeline draw groups.** GIVEN `MAX_MARKS` 32 marks in view, WHEN rendered, THEN Horror draw groups never exceed `HORROR_MARK_DRAW_GROUPS` 4. The guarantee at the minimum `D_marks` (4 to 6, where the knob table gives Horror none) is **NOT ASSESSED** until Pipeline and Failure & Trail rule on C-HE-31. *Covers HE-30.*
- **AC-HE-101 (lint) Sanity.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN scanned, THEN it holds no sanity reference, reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` block and neither defines nor uses `HORROR_NEAR_R` or `EXP_HORROR`. *Covers HE-32, HE-40.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: Sanity is `Status: Designed`; testable.)*
- **AC-HE-135 (lint + auto) The debug record is write-only.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN scanned, THEN `horror.view.debug` has exactly one writer (the run's publish step) and zero readers inside `src/` outside the Debug Overlay root and `tests/`; it is absent from the `horror.state` snapshot and from `state_hash`; GIVEN two replays of one seed, one with the debug record published and one with it suppressed, THEN `state_hash` is identical every 600 ticks; the record carries the target, goal room, fired guard, failed REACH condition and the `hold_runs`, `blocked_runs` and `horror_wedged` values for the run. *Covers HE-7, HE-41.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-102 (lint) Open Questions carry the corrections.** GIVEN this document, WHEN scanned, THEN the Open Questions table contains a row whose text includes "RUPTURE in the anchor ladder", a row that includes `RETURN_HOME` and "validation target (2)", a row for `tool_suppressant` marked "Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions)", and a row per open cross-GDD note; and every `(provisional — …)` string outside Open Questions names a dependency in the Dependencies table (0 unnamed). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was a review row with no scan.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

- **AC-HE-123 (review, BLOCKING gate) Art-bible refresh before the rig's asset-spec.** GIVEN a request to run `/asset-spec system:horror-entities` for the rig (H1, H2), WHEN the owed rows ART:542 ("Physical honesty"), ART 5.5 intro, PIPE:140 ("horror" removed from the occluder casters) and IDX:35 bullet 18 are checked, THEN each carries the no-occluder, no-shadow wording of HE-2 and the fiction gloss, or the asset-spec is refused; the rime and hairline sheets (H4, H5, H7) additionally need C-HE-21 decided. This is a precondition, not advice. *Covers HE-2, C-HE-1, C-HE-21.*
- **AC-HE-124 (review, gate; BLOCKING) No inert item in the MVP start kit.** Ruled 2026-10-27 (user ruling, Option A, X6). GIVEN the MVP start kit definition in Suit & Equipment (SE-11 slot 4, SE-14, SE-15), the MVP item list in the Item & Module Database and the HUD's MVP mark and state list, WHEN reviewed before implementation of any of them or of this block, THEN `tool_suppressant` is absent from all three while `repel(origin, T)` has no effect (AT-19), `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES` is deprecated in the registry, and Item DB AC-109 (the start kit has no consumer-less def) passes; a build whose start kit holds an item whose use changes nothing in the world fails the gate. If AT-19 is reversed at Vertical Slice the effect and the item arrive together. *Covers HE-39, C-HE-6.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

### Group Z: performance and soak

- **AC-HE-103 (spike) Per-run cost.** GIVEN a slice with one horror, 4 seats and a full mark ring on each reference machine, RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (`project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`; release-export build, `forward_plus`, vsync on, 120-frame warm-up, with the startup renderer and driver read-back recorded), pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK; the threshold stands *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*, WHEN `horror.step` is profiled over 10000 runs, THEN P99 is at most `HORROR_STEP_BUDGET_MS` 0.15 ms and the maximum is at most 2 ms (the kernel's P3 target share); the GDScript cost, the rig draw count (10 to 13 sprites in the Y-sorted layer) and per-instance shader parameters are spike parameters because `docs/engine-reference/godot/` has no `scripting.md`, `core.md` or 2D-skeleton coverage.
- **AC-HE-104 (auto) Per-run counts.** GIVEN any run, THEN `lux` reads ≤ 4, `solid_in` queries ≤ 5, expansions ≤ `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` 64, probes 0, mark inserts ≤ `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` 2, M17 calls ≤ `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 4, `apply_suit_damage` ≤ 1 per seat, dirty keys per tick ≤ `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256; and the serialised `horror.state` with a full ring (32 marks), 4 L3 entries and 16 `last_end_T` entries is at most 16 KiB `[PLACEHOLDER; the AC-HE-103 spike may lower it and records the measured size]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was "set from the spike" with no number)*.
- **AC-HE-105 (auto, soak) Repeated cycles.** GIVEN a headless run of 864 000 ticks (4 hours at `TICK_RATE` 60) of a scripted cycle of CALM 1200, TELL 600, BREACH 900 and SUBSIDING 1200 ticks (3900 ticks, about 221 cycles) with one lit seat that walks out of every REACH, THEN the **structure** of the serialised `horror.state` at cycle 221 equals its structure at the first cycle at which the fixture observes a full ring (it records that cycle and it must be at most cycle 20): the key set equals the committed manifest, every list length is at or below its cap (ring at most `MAX_MARKS`, each quota'd kind at most its quota, `gouged_seg_ids` at most `GOUGES_PER_EPOCH_MAX`, the disturbance list at most `L3_MAX_ACTIVE`, `last_end_T` at most `L3_MAX_TRACKED`) and the lengths equal those of the first full-ring cycle; **no byte size is compared** (a correct build varies in encoded size with integer magnitude); every `*_withheld` counter is 0, `mark_id` is strictly increasing with no reuse, `horror_wedged` is 0, and a second run of the same seed matches `state_hash` every 600 ticks *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: "accelerated 4-hour-equivalent" is now an exact tick count and cycle)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.

### Group M: legibility, feel and the visual half (BLOCKING; retained screenshots; lead sign-off)

**Validation targets** (Player Fantasy; creative-director, placeholders). (1) *Fair death:* at least 80 % of horror-caused deaths are described with a cause the player saw; below 60 % the telegraph or stalk is failing. (2) *Not an alarm monster:* in at least 70 % of sessions the player sees the horror or its fresh marks during CALM at least once. (3) *The dilemma exists:* at least half of testers asked "what did you do when it came?" describe turning the lamp off, abandoning a repair or closing a door. (4) *No hidden-number belief:* zero describe the horror "remembering" or "locking on" across a lamp-off. (5) *Marks read as web, not ship:* blind classification of rime against coolant frost is correct at least 90 % at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2`. (6) *Teeth:* in a replay of a breach on a clean ship the player loses at least one repair's worth of progress or one suit rung in at least 60 % of the breaches they choose to commit through lit. (7) *A CALM sighting changes behaviour:* at least 60 % of testers who saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM report that it changed what they did (route, lamp, or which job first); if it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` to 0 first and never relax HE-11 (AC-HE-122). **Sample rule:** every percentage threshold below needs N ≥ 10 testers (N ≥ 10 sessions for telemetry rows); a shorter run reads NOT ASSESSED. These rows are run first in playtest because they decide whether the numeric rows matter. **Evidence:** `production/qa/evidence/horror-entities/<ac-id>/`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Row types:** the Visual/Feel rows (AC-HE-107, 108, 109, 110, 112, 113, 117, 118; 96 for its pixel scan) and the UI row (AC-HE-119) require retained screenshots; the Playtest rows (AC-HE-106, 111, 114, 115, 116, 122) require session evidence (coded notes, rater agreement, telemetry) and no screenshots.

- **AC-HE-106 (manual, blind) Blind legibility of the telegraph (validation target 1).** GIVEN N >= 10 first-time players each experiencing a told breach with a lit seat and the horror approaching from behind the lamp cone, WHEN the REACH reaches `reach_end_T`, THEN the limb tip is visibly lit for the victim in every captured frame in all five facings at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` (the tip rests on P inside the victim's own light); at least `ceil(0.875 × N)` players (9 of 10) name the limb or the smear as the cause of a hit or a miss, coded by two raters with agreement of at least 0.8 (a third rater breaks ties); and, when at least 4 players were hit, at least 75 % of those hit call it avoidable (with fewer than 4 hit that clause reads NOT ASSESSED). If this fails the fallback is a larger `UNFOLD_LO` or a smaller `R_SNAP_PX` (first tweak 48), never a marker. *Covers HE-14, HE-F5, C-HE-9.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* Run with the final smear timing: the smear is laid at the hold stage, 25 to 50 ticks before the strike (HE-14), so a rater codes an answer naming the smear as post-hoc evidence of where the limb landed, never as the warning; the nine-of-ten count is of players who name the limb, or after the fact the smear or the STRUCK mark, as the cause. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the 87.5 % of 10 is now 9 of 10, with a rater rule and a minimum hit count)*
