- **AC-HE-107 (manual) The tip at the narrowest field.** GIVEN a seat aiming away from the horror at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` (including 1080p 384 × 216), WHEN a REACH is in the hold stage, THEN the tip pixel (the rig's published tip anchor) lies inside the viewport with a margin of at least `LIMB_TIP_MIN_PX` 4 px at all three steps in each of 8 aim directions (24 captures, 24 of 24 required); the share of captures in which the whole limb is on screen is recorded per step and not asserted; at k = 1 at least 90 % of 5 blind judges classify the frame as "limb extended" against "limb retracted". *Covers HE-F14, C-HE-23.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was "recorded" with no threshold)*
- **AC-HE-108 (manual) Silhouette and lit frame.** GIVEN the poses `rest`, `reach`, `move` and `snap`, WHEN shown at 25 % with no colour and no contour, THEN each passes the art-bible silhouette test and contains no closed circle, bilateral symmetry, four-limb pairs or held object; a Void Steel rig on a lit Graphite floor reads as a figure under each of the four beam shapes and the three ring ambients with an achromatic pass (measured, not judged: at 25 % scale and no colour the flood-fill count of enclosed regions is 0, the horizontal mirror overlap is below 85 % IoU, the limb count is not 4 and no limb pair is collinear within 15 degrees, and the relief passes carry at least 3 distinct levels per segment slice); and a blind tester separates a real still frame from a stage-2 "shadow with no occluder" in at least 15 of 20 trials (binomial p < 0.05 against 50 %) with hue removed. *Covers HE-40, ART 3.1, ART 4.4.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: silhouette tests are now measured)*
- **AC-HE-109 (manual, blind) Collision fixtures (validation target 5).** GIVEN FT AC-79 (frost fan against branching rime, Chemlight pool against rime, O2 mist against smear, RUPTURE trail against hairlines), WHEN shown in greyscale single frames at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` to N ≥ 10, THEN each pair is classified correctly at least 90 %, the lit rendered rime measures L\* 70 to 78 and hue at least 36 degrees from flesh, and the art-director signs off. *Covers HE-31.*
- **AC-HE-110 (manual) Hairlines are not trails.** GIVEN a STRIKE within `HULL_CONTACT_PX`, WHEN the result is shown, THEN radial hairlines with no hole at the centre and no stroke below 2 px sit at the base of the hull face while Failure & Trail's descriptor runs along the rib or seam, and the blind classification of AC-HE-96 (N ≥ 10, at least 90 % correct at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2`) passes with the art-director's sign-off. *Covers AC-HE-96.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-111 (manual, blind) The three "edge of my light" sources.** GIVEN a room with a TELL in progress and a horror present, WHEN N ≥ 10 testers watch, THEN at least 80 % separate "the lamp near the thing" (L3: local, irregular), "the dips on the beat" (Attention: global) and "the ship's power" (a Graph sag) by rhythm alone. *Covers HE-35.*
- **AC-HE-112 (manual + lint) Ingress and no fade.** GIVEN an ingress, egress, ageing and eviction, WHEN a 60 fps capture is stepped, THEN every Horror sprite alpha is 0 or 255 in every frame (lint: no tween on `modulate:a` in the presenter); where a wall face exists the rig emerges across its south edge; the ingress mark precedes it. *Covers V8, V9, V14.*
- **AC-HE-113 (manual) Reduced motion and reduced flashing.** GIVEN each accessibility setting on, WHEN the telegraph plays, THEN the unfold-hold-snap sequence is present and its pose-tag sequence (`reach_a`, `reach_b`, `reach_c`, `snap`) and stage lengths are identical to the setting-off run (0 differing poses), the idle sway amplitude is 0, the creep irregularity is at most one 15-degree quantum on one limb per pose step, the post-chain L3 factor never falls below 850 permille (AC-HE-134) and never below `MOD_FLOOR_PM` under reduced flashing; no Horror luminance change exceeds 3 Hz (method: a 10 s capture at 60 fps, count luminance sign changes of the Horror sprite and of any L3-dipped lamp, at most 6 per second); the achromatopsia and colourblind passes keep the telegraph (shape, motion, stillness) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: reduced motion is now a pose-tag and amplitude comparison)*.
- **AC-HE-114 (manual, telemetry) CALM presence (validation target 2).** GIVEN N ≥ 10 thirty-minute solo sessions, WHEN telemetry is read, THEN in at least 70 % the player saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM at least once (seen: on screen at least 1 s, logged as an event) and the horror never chose a step below `LURK_STANDOFF_PX`. If it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` first. *Covers HE-11, HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-115 (manual, telemetry) Teeth and the dilemma (validation targets 3, 4, 6).** GIVEN a replay of a breach on a clean ship, WHEN N ≥ 10 breaches that the player chose to commit through lit are counted (lamp on, in the locus body at BREACH entry, channelling a lit-only verb), THEN in at least 60 % the player loses one completed channel's progress or one suit rung; at least half of testers asked "what did you do when it came?" describe turning the lamp off, abandoning a repair or closing a door; zero describe it "remembering" or "locking on" across a lamp-off. *Covers HE-12, HE-13, HE-26.*
- **AC-HE-116 (manual) Game Feel: the strike is witnessed by the strike.** GIVEN a blind playtest of N ≥ 10, WHEN horror-caused deaths are described, THEN, given at least 10 horror-caused deaths, drawn organically or from the scripted cohort (the harness stands a seat still through three strikes at 10 distinct seeds; the organic death rate is recorded and not asserted; fewer than 10 deaths of either kind reads NOT ASSESSED), at least 80 % are described with a cause the player saw, coded by two raters with agreement of at least 0.8; strike one is witnessed by the snap pose, Audio's positional cue from `strike_T` and the STRUCK mark on the floor (a tester who returns to the spot reads it as "it got me here"); there is no hit-stop, screen shake, damage number or flash from any Horror source. *Covers HE-15, HE-38, validation target (1), Game Feel.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: minimum death count and rater agreement added)*
- **AC-HE-122 (manual, telemetry or interview) A CALM sighting changes behaviour (validation target 7).** GIVEN N ≥ 10 thirty-minute solo sessions, WHEN testers who saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM are asked on one neutral question "did seeing it change what you did next?", THEN at least 60 % report a change in route, lamp use or which job they took first because of it. If it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` to 0 first (OQ-1); CALM stays non-lethal (AC-HE-14) and HE-11 is not relaxed. *Covers HE-11, HE-20, Overview, Player Fantasy.*
- **AC-HE-117 (manual + auto) Presenter cadence.** GIVEN a 60 fps capture, WHEN stepped, THEN the rig pose changes only on ticks that are multiples of 5, segment rotation is a multiple of 15 degrees in every captured frame, position is interpolated and pose is not. *Covers HE-40, HE-F18.*
- **AC-HE-118 (manual) Dark, restore, unlit marks.** GIVEN marks and rig at 0 % ambient with no light in range, THEN neither is drawn (the rig and mark regions equal the surrounding unlit floor value, 0 differing pixels, and the hull-floor tint is not applied); the first frame after a restore into LURK, STALK, REACH (mid-hold) and RETREAT shows the pose tag equal to the pure function of `(state, state_enter_T, now)`, a mark count equal to the restored ring length, no transition frame (the pose tag does not change within the 5 ticks after the restore) and, for REACH, a remaining telegraph of exactly `reach_end_T − T_restore` ticks *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: each clause now has a measured pass condition)*. *Covers D-AD1, V19.*
- **AC-HE-119 (manual, UI) No Horror surface in the HUD.** GIVEN the HUD states captured by the HUD owner (default, each accessibility setting, lamp on and off, sanity-degraded) with a horror in view, WHEN screenshots are taken of each, THEN none shows a horror, a threat bar, a proximity indicator or a direction, and a lint finds no HUD asset naming a horror. *Covers UI Requirements, AT-24.* *(HUD & Suit Layer is designed (`hud-and-suit-layer.md`); the state list is the HUD owner's UX-spec list of at least 6 states: default, each of the 3 accessibility settings, lamp on and off, sanity-degraded; fewer captured states read NOT ASSESSED.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-HE-120 (lint) Stale-token scan.** GIVEN this GDD and every file that cites it (Attention, Emitter, Lighting, Suit, Interaction, Failure & Trail, Item DB, Derelict Authoring, Save/Load), WHEN scanned whole-word and case-sensitive against the list at the end of Cross-References with the whitelist file `tests/lint/horror_entities_stale_tokens.whitelist` (one entry per allowed quotation or negation, each with its line and a reason) ("near" meaning within 60 characters), THEN there are zero hits outside the whitelist. The exemption is checked by review. *Covers the scan list.*

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **The CHILL degrade path (C-HE-28).** Retired 2026-10-27 (design review): Attention narrowed AT:373 to the harm kinds, so no contingency criterion remains.
- **`HORROR_RESTORE_RNG_COUNTER` (C-HE-30).** Closed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: HE-41 defines the trigger (negative, non-integer or at or above `RESTORE_KEY_STRIDE`) and AC-HE-77 has its crafted save.
- **`D_marks` 4 to 6 (C-HE-31).** The knob table gives Horror zero draw groups there; no guarantee is assertable until Pipeline and Failure & Trail rule (AC-HE-100).
- **Branch-angle spacing (C2).** The art-director's "no two adjacent spacings within 5 degrees" has no source in HE-31 or FT:1427; only seed purity is asserted.
- **L3 duration versus the multiple-of-5 lint (C-HE-29).** HE-4 now exempts `L3_DUR_LO` and `L3_DUR_HI`; AC-HE-65 asserts the 18 to 30 range.
- **Provisional rows.** Refreshed 2026-10-27 (design review): AC-HE-30 to 33, 38, 48, 54, 63, 64, 71, 74, 99 and 101 are re-tagged testable because every neighbour they waited on is `Status: Designed` and the reads are published. Still not assessable: AC-HE-100 at `D_marks` 4 to 6 (C-HE-31), the Vertical Slice class (a) rows of AC-HE-63 and 64 beyond the Filter stub, and AC-HE-119 until the HUD owner's state list is captured; AC-HE-124 is now ruled BLOCKING *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. AC-HE-103 and AC-HE-131 are pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12), and AC-HE-127 and AC-HE-128 run against Layout's published `door_passage` (LY-28, AC-180) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12; cross-GDD fix)*
- **Reference hardware.** Resolved 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12 (user delegated all remaining calls, including naming the reference machine): AC-HE-103 and AC-HE-131 run on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK on a release-export build (`project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`; Open Question 35 closed) and are pending spike measurement, not blocked *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*.
- **Manual sample sizes.** Group M percentages assume N ≥ 10; the minimum N and the result when N is short (NOT ASSESSED) are stated once above the group.
- **Hold-stage pose count at the lower bound.** The art-director's "last 30 % of the unfold, not under 27 ticks" holds at `UNFOLD_LO` 95 (28.5 ticks, 6 poses); it is asserted by AC-HE-106 and the presenter stub of the Visual/Audio section, not by a separate row.

## Open Questions

Owner: **user** = a creative or scope decision the user (via the creative-director) must make; **agent** = a specialist or neighbour-GDD author; **GDD** = this document's author. Target: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the named neighbour), **ADR** (an architecture decision). Rows 1 to 7 are the user-decision items; the autonomous run proposed the answer shown and proceeded on it. `[CUT-FIRST]` marks an item that is the first thing to cut if scope tightens.

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`RETURN_HOME` and validation target (2).** Default 1: the horror returns to derelict B after a breach, so a CALM sighting needs the player to be near it. The creative-director leans to 0 (it lurks where it last breached). If fewer than 70 % of 30-minute solo sessions see the horror or a fresh mark in CALM (AC-HE-114), flip this knob first. Ruled at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate: keep default 1 for MVP (a home body on derelict B maps risk to place, Pillar 5, and keeps the home-ship from being permanently haunted before playtest data exists); flip to 0 first if validation target (2) or the CALM behaviour-change target (7, AC-HE-122) fails | user | playtest |
| 2 | **RUPTURE in the anchor ladder.** At the default `ANCHOR_REACH_GOUGES` 1 an anchor REACH (no lit seat) lays hairlines and one GOUGE and never a RUPTURE, so a RUPTURE is reachable only when a lit seat stands beside a hull face that already carries this epoch's GOUGE (HE-16, C-HE-24). Should the dark-seat answer also risk a RUPTURE after a GOUGE? Proposed: no at MVP. *Design review 2026-10-27:* RUPTURE is now withheld on any strike that hits a seat (HE-16 (2), E-HE-56), which removes the CRITICAL-plus-staggered-plus-breach compound; it stays reachable only on a miss beside a gouged face. The alternative the reviewers raised, forbidding RUPTURE on every seat REACH, would make RUPTURE unreachable at MVP (the anchor REACH never ruptures) and is a scope change; Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions): the miss-only rule is confirmed (RUPTURE withheld on any strike that hits a seat, reachable only on a miss beside a gouged face); the alternative, forbidding RUPTURE on every seat REACH, is rejected because it would make RUPTURE unreachable at MVP *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (closed) | closed |
| 3 | **`tool_suppressant` in the MVP start kit (C-HE-6).** Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions): Option A (X6): `tool_suppressant` is removed from the MVP start kit (SE-11 slot 4, SE-14, SE-15), the Item DB MVP list and the HUD; HE-39's `repel` stays a dormant `[CUT-FIRST]` interface; AC-HE-124 is BLOCKING; Item DB AC-109 passes; the registry `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES` is retired (status deprecated). The question was whether an inert tool with 3 charges (a false affordance against Pillar 1's legibility and the 'information is the reward' contract) belongs in the kit; Option B (keep it, AC-HE-124 ADVISORY, Pillar 1 waiver) is not taken. Suit, Item DB, the HUD and Attention apply their own rows, then the consistency check is rerun; if AT-19 is reversed the effect and the item arrive together *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (closed) | closed (rows owed by Suit, Item DB, HUD, Attention) |
| 4 | **Rime White-Grey `#B0B8C0` (C-HE-21).** A hue-less rime at rendered L\* 70 to 78 has no palette hex; the palette lock would fail the rime and hairline sheets. Decide: add the entry (proposed), or read FT:1427 as a rendered-appearance range and exempt Hull Grey for a floor mark. Blocks asset-spec for rime and hairlines, not this GDD | user (creative-director, art-director) | art bible, `/asset-spec` |
| 5 | **A body witness for strike one (Suit OQ-19).** Suit's STRAINED rung is inert by design, so strike one is witnessed by the strike itself (snap pose, positional cue) and, in Horror's own domain, by the STRUCK mark laid at P only when a seat was hit (CD-GDD-ALIGN: the floor now differs after a hit and a miss, so Pillar 1's "the pipe" is met in the world as well as on the instrument). This is not a Pillar 1 violation (C-HE-25); Suit's OQ-19 sprite witness stays the reversal path if validation target (1) fair death fails (AC-HE-106, AC-HE-116). Proposed: no row owed unless the target fails | user | playtest |
| 6 | **A CALM presence aboard the home ship after a home-ship breach (HE-20).** The horror comes to the home ship through the hull in a TELL; does it linger aboard in CALM when `RETURN_HOME` is 0, or does the ship feel safe once it leaves? Proposed: it returns after `RETURN_DELAY_TICKS` 600. Ruled at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate: keep default `RETURN_HOME` 1 for MVP (see row 1). **Resolved in the design review (2026-10-27):** the default stands, and the question no longer masks the cry-wolf defect: the horror departs an unloaded body at every TELL elsewhere under either setting (HE-20, guard 2, AC-HE-125), so a told breach always brings it; `RETURN_HOME` 0 only changes where it lurks afterwards | user (closed) | closed |
| 7 | **The carrier is caught by design (C-HE-8).** A seat hauling cargo needs 72 ticks to clear the disc and has 40; the answer is to drop the cargo. Is this the intended price of hauling (Pillar 5 risk), with a smaller `R_SNAP_PX` (first tweak 48) as the lever? Proposed: yes. *Design review 2026-10-27:* the figure is explicit (a carrier who keeps the cargo is struck on 7 of 18 draws, 38.9 %, HE-F5), the stated counterplay is to drop the cargo (Interaction's `drop` is a TAP, lit-exempt and uninterruptible, IA-28, rejected only with `OBSTRUCTED` when no free cell exists), AC-HE-132 asserts the drop timeline, and the telegraph is **not** tied to `V_CARRY` (a carrier-length telegraph would be a new design). **Ask of Interaction:** may a player `drop` fall back to the foot origin when every neighbouring cell is occupied, as the forced drop of a downed actor does? **Ask of the Controller:** is the walk gait restored on the tick `clear_carry_ref` lands? Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions): 'drop the cargo' is confirmed as the intended price of hauling (a carrier who keeps the cargo is struck on 7 of 18 draws, 38.9 %); `R_SNAP_PX` stays 72 and 48 is the ruled lever (2 of 18 draws, 11.1 %; derivation in HE-F5). The two asks of Interaction and the Controller above are not closed by this ruling and stay owed (row 30) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
